Diesel pressure                                                       Btu in 2027, down 9% from 2026.
    Distillate markets are at the center of the current price

rally, EIA said. US retail diesel prices averaged $6.29/gallon in
September, and the EIA expects prices to remain above $6/
gal through October before gradually easing to an average of
around $4.50/gal in 2027. East Coast distillate inventories stood
32% below their five-year seasonal average in September and
are forecast to remain between 20% and 30% below average
throughout the winter -- a persistent supply deficit that will keep
heating oil and diesel markets exposed to further shocks.

Trump signed an executive order temporarily lifting restrictions
